TOWN OF RIVERHEAD 
 
TB Resolution 2026-715 
 
RESOLUTION TO AUTHORIZE AND APPROVE AGREEMENT WITH RIVERHEAD AMBULANCE CORP. 
SUBJECT TO PERMISSIVE REFERENDUM 
 
Councilman Kern offered the following resolution,  
which was seconded by Councilwoman Merrifield 
 
          WHEREAS, the Town of Riverhead is a municipal corporation organized and existing 
under the Laws of the State of New York since 1792; and 
  
WHEREAS, on August 19, 1986, the Town Board authorized the issuance of 
$114,000.00 serial bonds and appropriation of $6000.00 current funds to pay costs for the 
construction of a three bay ambulance storage barn on Columbus Avenue; and 
  
WHEREAS, on July 19, 1988, the Town of Riverhead authorized the issuance of 
$218,500 serial bonds and $11,500.00 capital notes to pay additional costs for the construction 
of the above-described barn on town owned land at 1111 Osborne Avenue with said total 
project construction anticipated at a cost of $350,000.00; and 
 
WHEREAS, on or about October of 1999, the Town of Riverhead Ambulance District was 
established and a map and plan including, a description of the boundaries of the district; 
estimated costs; no anticipated or scheduled capital improvements; anticipated contract 
agreement for services; and 
  
WHEREAS, since July of 1988 and after establishment of the Riverhead Ambulance 
District, the Ambulance District has expended funds for the improvement of the ambulance barn 
at 1111 Osborne Avenue and the small substation on Manor Lane, Jamesport, ie. Resolution 
1021 adopted on November 18, 2008; and 
  
WHEREAS, RVAC seeks to continue its ambulance services with the Ambulance District 
and seeks to enter into a formal lease agreement for the property at 1111 Osborne Avenue; 
 
WHEREAS, pursuant to Town Law 64 (2) a Town may convey or lease real property in 
the name of the Town, upon a resolution subject to a permissive referendum.  
  
NOW, THEREFORE, BE IT RESOLVED, that this resolution is subject to Permissive 
Referendum as provided in Article 7 of the New York State Town Law and Municipal Home Rule 
Law and that the Town Clerk is directed to publish and post a public notice, an abstract of which 
follows; and be it further,  
 
RESOLVED, the Town Board, be and hereby authorizes the Supervisor to execute a 
lease agreement between the Town of Riverhead/Riverhead Ambulance District and Riverhead 
Volunteer Ambulance Corp. in substantially the form attached; and be it further, 
 
 RESOLVED, that the Town Clerk is authorized to forward a certified copy of this 
resolution to the Office of the Town Attorney, and; and be it further, 
Page 158 of 218
 
RESOLVED, that all Town Hall departments may review and obtain a copy of this 
resolution from the electronic storage device and, if needed, a certified copy of same may be 
obtained from the office of the Town Clerk.

March 12, 2009; and most recent Resolution #                adopted on                    ;  
the Town and RVAC have entered into agreements for ambulance services to 
benefit the Riverhead Ambulance District providing services twenty-four (24) hours per 
Page 162 of 218
day, seven (7) days per week, fifty-two (52) weeks per year for each and every year 
covered by the agreement and such agreements also acknowledge and do not restrict 
RVAC from entering into agreements for non-emergency ambulances services or 
contracting with other municipal entities; and 
 
While some improvements have been made to the ambulance barn since original 
construction, the changes or improvements were minor i.e. some flooring repairs, 
cabinetry and air conditioning. The improvements did not expand the ambulance barn 
nor undertake significant renovations to address and necessary to accommodate 
staffing, storage of equipment and medications due to increased population and 
increase in emergency call volume and response; and  
 
As reflected in census data Riverhead between 2000 and 2020 Riverhead’s population 
grew by over 29.5 percent and according to census data Riverhead’s population was 
27,605 residents in 2020 and according to 2024 census Riverhead’s population has 
grown to 36,067 residents; and 
 
As the Town and its taxpayers have and continue to face budgetary and financial 
constraints and based upon commitment to other capital projects, the Town has 
determined that due to the age of the existing ambulance barn investment into 
renovation and expansion is not cost effective nor is commitment to a capital project 
necessary to undertake construction of a new ambulance barn feasible at this time; and 
 
In light of the above, RVAC expressed its intent and did embark on a campaign to 
fundraise for funding to construct an ambulance facility in place of the existing 
ambulance barn that would serve and benefit RVAC and its volunteers with work space, 
education and training room, storage areas for vehicles, equipment and supplies and of 
course and perhaps most significantly, increase and improve emergency ambulance 
services to the Ambulance Districts and its community; and 
 
As described above, as the Town (“Lessor) is the owner of real property located at 1111 
Osborne Avenue, Riverhead, NY 11901 (hereinafter referred to as “subject property”), 
the Town seeks to enter into a long term lease agreement with RVAC “Lessee” with the 
terms of said lease to permit Lessee to demolish existing structure; make improvements 
including construction of new ambulance barn, other site improvements, subject to 
certain terms and conditions, including but not limited to, assignment and termination 
provisions with requirement for continued use for emergency ambulance services 
necessary to serve the Riverhead Ambulance District; costs (be it related to RVAC 
payment of bond principal and interest if financed) shall not be a charge against the 
town nor serve as a budget increase for annual emergency ambulance services 
agreement between the Town and RVAC.   
 
In consideration of the mutual covenants and agreements stated, the Lessor leases to 
the Lessee and the Lessee leases from the Lessor for the purposes established the 
land described as the Subject Property upon the following terms and conditions: 
 
 
ARTICLE I. Definitions

Section 1.1. Singular, Plural and Gender.  Any word contained in the text of this Lease

meanings attributed to them in this Section: 
 
(a) "Building" means the approximately ____________ square foot ambulance barn 
(also hereinafter referred to as ambulance facility to be constructed by the Lessee as 
part of the Improvements on the Subject Property pursuant to the terms of Article IV. 
(A copy of the proposed improvements, including floor plans and site improvements 
is annexed hereto as Exhibit “A” and made a part hereof).  
 
(b) "Certificate of Occupancy" means the certificate issued by the building official 
which permits the use of a building and land in furtherance of emergency ambulance 
services necessary to serve the Riverhead Ambulance District and such other uses 
set forth in the Contract between Riverhead Ambulance District and Riverhead 
Volunteer Ambulance Corps, Inc. “RVAC” and in compliance with the Town Code for 
the Town of Riverhead. 
 
(c) "Construction Contract" or "Construction Documents" means the contracts, plans, 
and specifications for the construction of the Building and the Improvements 
involved with the development of the land as well as any supplemental agreements, 
which shall be supplemented with more detailed and final plans as they are available 
and approved by the Lessor. 
 
(d) "Bond Issuance Entity aka Bond Purchaser" means any financing the Lessor 
secures for purpose of securing funding related to completion of construction of the 
Building and the Improvements (or any part of them), to be constructed by the 
Lessee upon the Subject Property all to be repaid by Lessee in installments equal to 
annual principal and interest payments due for repayment to Bond Issuance Entity 
upon the Subject Property under these terms. 
 
(e) "Control" (including correlated meanings such as the terms "controlling," 
"controlled by," and "under common control with"), as used with respect to any 
entity, means the possession, directly or indirectly or the power to direct or cause the 
direction of the management decisions and policies of that entity, whether through 
the ownership of voting securities, beneficial interests or by contract or otherwise. 
 
(f) "Disposition" (including the correlated meaning "dispose") means any direct or 
indirect lease, assignment, transfer, distribution to shareholders, partners or 
beneficial owners of any Person or other disposition by private sale, public offering 
or by enforcement of any pledge or by gift, by bequest, or under the laws of descent 
and distribution, or for value or otherwise whether direct or indirect, voluntary or 
involuntary, or by operation of law. 
 
(g) "Event of Default" means the occurrence of any action specified in Section 26.1.

the first Lease Year shall commence upon the first day of the calendar month next 
following the date of the commencement of the term. Each succeeding Lease Year

ARTICLE II. The Demise 
 
Section 2.1. The Demise. The Lessor, for and in consideration of the covenants and 
agreements made by the Lessee to be kept, observed and performed, does demise and 
lease to the Lessee, and the Lessee leases from the Lessor, the Subject Property, 
subject to the Permitted Exceptions, AND TO HAVE AND TO HOLD the Subject 
Property for a term of twenty five (25) years commencing on the date of this Lease 
Agreement, being the first day of the first Lease Year. 
 
Section 2.2. Building and Improvements to the property shall be the property of Lessee  
so long as this Lease Agreement or any lease given in substitution of this Lease 
Agreement, pursuant to Section 6.3, remains in force, any Improvements including the 
Building constructed by the Lessee on the Subject Property (specifically excluding the 
Public Improvements constructed by the Lessee, which shall become the property of the 
Lessor, special district or public utility as the case may be upon completion and 
acceptance) and on termination of this Lease Agreement or Lessor’s exercise of the 
reverter provision, the Building and Improvements shall become the sole property of the 
Lessor in fee simple and free and clear of all encumbrances excepting only the lien of 
taxes assessed but not yet due and payable (for which the Lessee shall remain 
obligated to pay to the extent that they are allocable to the period prior to the 
termination of this Lease Agreement and subject to the Lessor's rights and remedies in 
the event of the termination of this Lease Agreement because of Lessee's default), the 
Permitted Exceptions (except those acts or judgments by or against the Lessee or 
anyone claiming under it) to 
make the Lessor whole on account of the Lessee's default and to make a new Lease 
Page 167 of 218
Agreement of the Subject Property, the Building, and Improvements with the Lessor for 
a period equivalent to the balance of the term of this Lease Agreement then remaining. 
 
Section 2.3. Covenants To Perform. This Lease Agreement is made upon the above  
and the following covenants and conditions, each of which the party bound by such 
covenants and conditions agrees to perform, irrespective of whether the particular 
provision is in the form of a covenant, an agreement, a condition, a direction or 
otherwise, and each party agrees to provide the other party with documents or further 
assurances as may be required to carry out the expressed intention of the parties. 
 
Section 2.4. Title Insurance. Not Applicable 
 
ARTICLE III. Term 
 
Section 3.1. Term. The term of this Lease Agreement shall commence on the date of 
this Lease Agreement and shall end on the last day of the twenty fifth consecutive year. 
The Lease Agreement shall automatically five successive ten year terms each from and 
after the expiration of the initial term hereof (said renewal periods of ten years each 
being hereinafter sometimes referred to respectively as the "First Renewal Term," the 
"Second Renewal Term," and the "Third Renewal Term"…), provided that with respect 
to each renewal period this lease shall be in full force and effect immediately prior to the 
date of the commencement of such renewal period. If the Lessee desires to exercise 
the option herein to renew the lease for the First Renewal Period and any such other 
renewal term, it shall give the Lessor written notice of its intention so to do on or 
before one year (365 days) prior to the expiration of the initial term hereof or any 
subsequent renewal term. The renewal terms shall be on the same terms, covenants 
and conditions as in this lease, except for provisions identifying and describing changes 
to terms during the original Lease Term or an Amendment to any such term of the 
original Lease or Renewal Term as negotiated by and between the Lessor and Lessee 
and approved by Resolution of the Town Board, as governing body of the Town of 
Riverhead and Riverhead Ambulance District.  
 
ARTICLE IV. Possession and Construction of Improvements 
 
Section 4.1. Delivery of Possession of Subject Property.  Upon the execution and 
delivery of this Lease Agreement, the Lessor has delivered possession to the Lessee of 
the Subject Property. 
 
Section 4.2. Lessee's Construction Obligations.  At the sole cost and expense of the 
Lessee and pursuant to proper building permits (including demolition permit, foundation 
permit, etc) issued by the Lessor in the manner provided by law and Code of the Town 
of Riverhead, the Lessee shall construct and develop on the Subject Property: 
 
(a) A building of approximately ____________ square feet of dedicated and 
designed to house, store, and service emergency medical vehicles such as 
ambulances, offices and meeting/training areas, restrooms, storage for supplies and 
other improvements to provide operational support for personnel and dispatch of 
emergency medical services, in accord with the sketch plan attached and made a 
Page 168 of 218
part of this Lease Agreement as Exhibit “A”  
 
(b) In respect to the Lessee's use and occupation of the Subject Property (and the 
subsurface of it) during the progress and period of construction or by anyone acting 
under the Lessee—for all acts other than the acts of the Lessor, its officers, agents, 
and employees—the Lessee covenants to indemnify, defend and hold harmless the 
Lessor and its agents and employees from and against all claims and demands 
whatsoever for loss or damage, including property damage, personal injury and 
wrongful death arising out of entry or construction and development of the Subject 
Property and the Public Improvements or in the performance of this Lease 
Agreement by the Lessee, its agents, employees, contractors, subcontractors or 
invitees or as a result of any incident, fire or other casualty in respect of the Subject 
Property, or any failure by the Lessee to keep the Subject Property, or any 
Improvements on it, in a safe condition, and furnish the Lessor with the form of 
comprehensive general public liability insurance policy and fire and casualty policy 
for the amounts and subject to the provisions described in Article XII below.  
 
Section 4.3. Time for Commencement and Completion of Construction of 
Improvements. The Lessee agrees for itself, its permitted successors and assigns, and 
every permitted successor in interest that, subject to Unavoidable Delays, the Lessee, 
within three months of the date of this Lease Agreement or three months from receipt of 
all state, county, town permits and approvals (demolition permit, foundation permit, 
building permit, and any such other permit i.e. road opening permit required pursuant to 
Town Code), shall promptly commence and diligently prosecute to full completion the 
development of the Building and Improvements upon the Subject Property, including all 
the Public Improvements, and shall  substantially complete all construction within one 
year after the date of this Lease Agreement, subject to Unavoidable Delays. In the 
event of Unavoidable Delays, the date for commencement and/or date for Substantial 
Completion shall be correspondingly set back by the same number of days involved in 
the period of Unavoidable Delays. 
 
Section 4.4. Manner of Construction of Improvements. 
 
(a) Upon the Lessee's payment of all applicable permit fees and/or waiver of such 
fees by Lessor, proof of insurance, and provided the construction plans for the 
Building and Improvements comply with all applicable state, county and town codes, 
rules and regulations and: 
 
(b) The construction of the Improvements on the Subject Property shall be in 
accordance with the building plans and specifications approved by the Planning and 
Building Department of the Town of Riverhead; 
 
(c) The construction of all Improvements shall be performed in accordance with the 
provisions of all applicable federal, state and local laws, ordinances, rules, 
regulations, and other legal requirements; and 
 
(d) The Construction Contract and all subcontracts for the construction of the 
Improvements shall provide, and the Lessee shall require, that all contractors and 
Page 169 of 218
subcontractors furnish contractor's and subcontractor's affidavits in the form 
provided by the Office of the Town Attorney` and that waivers of lien from all persons 
who have performed work and labor, furnished services, or supplied equipment, 
materials or supplies in connection with the construction of the Improvements be 
required for all interim and final payments made. 
 
(e) In all events, the Lessee will pay and discharge to Lessor all costs, expenses and 
liabilities whatsoever arising out of the construction of the Building and 
Improvements and will keep the Subject Property free and clear of any and all liens 
and claims in any way arising out of the construction of the Building and 
Improvements on the Subject Property.  
 
Section 4.5. Rights of Access to Property. 
 
(a) The Lessor reserves for itself and any public utility company, as may be 
appropriate, the right of ingress to and egress from the Subject Property at all 
reasonable times for the purposes of reconstructing, maintaining or servicing the 
public utilities, if any, located within the boundary lines of the Subject Property, and 
to operate, service, maintain, repair, replace and reconstruct the Public 
Improvements provided, however, that any entry shall, at all times, be conducted in 
a reasonable manner and without any undue interruption or interference with the 
business and activities of the Lessee and any Sublessees. 
 
(b) The Lessee shall not construct any permanent Improvements over or within the 
boundary lines of any easement for public utilities unless previously approved by the 
Lessor and any applicable utility company; the Lessor agrees to cooperate with the 
Lessee in an attempt to obtain permission from any public or private agency or 
company to construct any permanent Improvements over easements if and to the 
extent required by the Lessee and necessary for better implementation of this Lease 
Agreement. 
 
(c) After the date of this Lease Agreement and during the construction of the 
Building and Improvements, the Lessee shall permit representatives of the Lessor 
access to the Subject Property at all reasonable times as the Lessor deems 
necessary for purposes of this Lease Agreement including, but not limited to, 
inspection of all Work being performed in connection with the construction of the 
Building and Improvements. No compensation shall be payable nor shall any charge 
be made in any manner by anyone for the access provided in this Section 4.5.

the date of this Lease Agreement) in a fund designated by Lessee and limited to

Lessee shall provide a copy Lessor with a copy of invoice, together with certificate, 
release and waiver as set forth in (1) and (2) above prior to remittance of payment to 
contractor or subcontractor. 
 
(b) Surveys. Within 30 days after the foundations for the Improvements have been 
installed, the Lessee shall furnish the Lessor with a survey by a registered land

constructed by the Lessee, for itself, to be within the perimeter lines of the Subject 
Property, and the Public Improvements to be within the subsurface and outside of 
the perimeter lines of the Subject Property. 
 
(c) If the Lessee fails to pay to the Lessor any fees or other sums due within the time 
fixed, or fails after 30 days' notice to pay to the Lessor sums and payments set forth

in 4.6(a) or any taxes due and unpaid, or fails to reimburse the Lessor within 60 days

Lessee shall be deemed in default and Lessor may take any such action set forth 
under Article XXVI of this Lease Agreement. 
 
 
Section 4.7. Use of the Lessor as Trustee For Costs for Completion of Construction In 
the event the Lessee shall require funds to supplement RVAC Private Funding needed 
to satisfy Lessee’s obligation for completion of Construction Improvements, Lessor may 
Bond or issue a Bond Anticipation Note for funds needed under this provision to satisfy 
Lessee’s obligation under provisions above. Lessee shall be responsible for any and all 
payments of principal and interest related thereto and Lessee shall make payments 
pursuant to the schedule prepared and approved by the Lessor’s Financial 
Administrator. The Lessee's obligations under this Article, or any other applicable 
provision of this Lease Agreement, require the Lessee to make the deposits with the 
Lessor who shall be deemed a trustee of the funds, and the Lessee shall have the same 
duties requirement for payment to contractor or subcontractor and  to examine or 
inquire into the propriety of the issuance of any architect's certificate or affidavit which 
shall be submitted to Lessor as a basis for it to make payments from the schedule of 
payments. Notwithstanding the above,  the Lessor as Trustee shall not be responsible 
for the proper application of any funds after the funds shall have been paid out by it 
pursuant to the provisions of this Lease Agreement.  
 
Section 4.8. Certificates of Occupancy.  Promptly after Substantial Completion of the 
Building and the Improvements, the Town Board, as governing body of the Town of 
Riverhead and Riverhead Ambulance District, Lessor, together with the Building 
Department and Planning Department, will consider and pass upon acceptance of the 
Improvements in accordance with those provisions of this Lease Agreement relating 
solely to the obligations of the Lessee to construct the Improvements (including the 
dates for beginning and completion), and subject to Lessee's rights under Section 4.3 
above, and then the Lessor shall furnish the Lessee with a Certificate of Occupancy. 
The Certificate shall be, and it shall be so provided in it, a conclusive determination of 
satisfaction and termination of the agreements and covenants in this Lease Agreement 
with respect to the obligations of the Lessee to construct the Improvements. Any 
certification and determination shall not constitute evidence of payment of the costs of 
the Work or compliance with or satisfaction of any obligation of the Lessee to any 
contractor or subcontractor. 
 
ARTICLE V. Rent 
 
Section 5.1. It is agreed that Lessee shall owe rent equal to any and all payments of 
principal and interest related to Bond or issue a Bond Anticipation Note thereto and paid 
pursuant to the schedule prepared and approved by the Lessor’s Financial 
Administrator. In addition, as and for agreement for rent, Lessee shall bear all costs of 
required improvements, including but not limited to water and sewer; compliance with all 
provisions of the Town Code for the Town of Riverhead and any restrictions and 
conditions pursuant to the applicable Board or Department responsible for review and 
Page 172 of 218
approval of plans; and proper and satisfactory services provided pursuant to and set 
forth in Contract for Services between Riverhead Ambulance District and RVAC. In the 
event that Lessee secures Private Funding to fully complete the project and no such 
Bond or BAN is required, the rent of principal and interest shall no longer be applicable 
as and for rent., however Lessee shall remain responsible for satisfaction for all costs of 
required improvements; compliance with all provisions of the Town Code for the Town 
of Riverhead and any restrictions and conditions pursuant to the applicable Board or 
Department responsible for review and approval of plans; and proper and satisfactory 
services provided pursuant to and set forth in Contract for Services between Riverhead 
Ambulance District and RVAC. In addition, in the event that Lessee secures Private 
Funding without requirement for Bond or Ban, Lessor reserves the right to negotiate a 
schedule of rent payments either in this Lease Agreement or Contract by and between 
Riverhead Ambulance District and RVAC. 
 
 
ARTICLE VI. Nonencumbrance of Subject Property  
 
Section 6.1. Limitation Upon Encumbrance of Subject Property. Covenant Against 
Encumbrances. Prior to the completion of the Improvements and issuance of a 
Certificate of Occupancy and except as provided for in the provisions of this Lease 
Agreement,  the Lessee covenants and agrees for itself, its permitted successors and 
assigns, and every permitted successor in interest to the Subject Property (collectively 
for purposes of this Article VI the "Lessee"), not to engage in any financing or other 
transaction creating any Mortgage or other encumbrance or lien upon the Subject 
Property whether by express agreement or operation of law, and not to place upon the 
Subject Property, or suffer to be placed upon the Subject Property, any lien or other 
encumbrance, other than a lien upon the Subject Property for current real estate taxes 
and assessments, levied but not then due and payable, and not to suffer any levy or 
attachment to be made on the Subject Property. Any Mortgage, encumbrance or lien 
shall be deemed a violation of this covenant on the date of its execution or filing of 
record regardless of whether or when it is foreclosed or otherwise enforced. 
 
ARTICLE VII. Additional Conditions of Lease Agreement and Restrictions  
Upon Use of Subject Property 
 
For the Lessee itself and every other Person who is a beneficiary of the Lessee or a 
permitted successor in interest in or to any part of the Subject Property and who is able 
to control the leasing or use of the Subject Property this Lease Agreement, in addition to 
all other Permitted Exceptions, conditions and restrictions described in this Lease 
Agreement or in any Exhibit to it, shall be subject to the following covenants and 
agreements which shall run with the Subject Property. 
 
Section 7.1. Certain Conditions of Leasing. The Lessee and other Person shall: 
 
(a) Devote the Subject Property only to those uses specified in this Lease 
Agreement and allowed by applicable laws, rules and regulations; 
 
(b) Not discriminate in violation of any applicable federal, state or local laws,

ordinances or regulations upon the basis of race, color, religion, sex, or national 
origin in the sale, lease or rental, or in the use or occupancy of the Subject Property, 
or any Improvements erected or to be erected on it or on any part of it; 
 
(c) Diligently prosecute the construction of the Improvements as provided (including 
the Public Improvements) and the payment for the Improvements and that the 
construction shall, in any event, be begun and completed in the period of time 
specified in Section 4.3, subject to Unavoidable Delays, and, except with the written 
approval of the Lessor (which approval shall not be withheld unreasonably), no 
changes shall be made in the Improvements which would not be in conformity with 
the terms of this Lease Agreement or constitute a major change in the 
Improvements or in the utilization of the Subject Property;

The covenants of this Section shall not be construed to restrict the Lessee from seeking 
partial or pro rata assessments of taxes for any period or periods during which the land

Section 7.2. Conditions Subsequent; Termination of Lease Agreement. The Lessee 
acknowledges the following conditions subsequent to this date: Without limitation of any 
other right or remedy of the Lessor, in the event the Lessee fails to remedy or abrogate 
any default, failure, violation or other action or inaction under this Article VII within the 
period and the manner specified in Article XXVI of the Lease Agreement, the Lessor at 
its option may cancel the leasehold estate to the Subject Property in the Lessee and all 
of the rights and interests in and to the Subject Property leased under the Lease 
Agreement to the Lessee, including the Building and Improvements shall revert to the 
Lessor.  
 
Without limiting any of the above, the Lessor and its successors and assigns shall be 
deemed beneficiaries of the covenants provided in this Article VII, both in its own right 
and for the purpose of protecting the interest of the community and other parties, 
whether public or private, in whose favor and for whose benefit the covenants shall be 
deemed provided. These covenants shall run in favor of the Lessor for the entire period 
during which each covenant shall be in force and effect without regard to whether the 
Lessor has at any time been, remains, or is an owner of any land or interest in it to or in 
favor of which the covenant relates. The Lessor shall also have the right, upon breach 
of any covenant, to exercise all rights or remedies and maintain actions or suits at law 
and equity or other proper proceedings to enforce the curing of any breach. 
 
ARTICLE VIII. Prohibitions Against Assignment and Transfers of Subject Property 
 
Section 8.1. Representations as to Redevelopment. The Lessee represents and agrees 
for itself, and its permitted successors and assigns, that its leasing of the Subject 
Property, and its other undertakings pursuant to this Lease Agreement are, and will be 
used, solely for the purpose of redevelopment of the Subject Property and its described 
permitted and intended use, and, not for speculation in land holding. The Lessee further 
Page 174 of 218
recognizes that, in view of:  
 
(a) The public aids and commitments that have been made available by law and by 
the Lessor for the purpose of making this redevelopment possible; and 
 
(b) the fact that any direct or indirect transfer of any beneficial interest in the Lessee 
or of a substantial part of it, or any other act or transaction involving or resulting in a 
significant change in the ownership or distribution of interests or with respect to the 
identity of the parties in control of the Lessee, is for practical purposes a transfer or 
Disposition of the leasehold in the Subject Property then leased by the Lessee; the 
qualifications and identity of the Lessee and their present beneficial owners, partners 
or venturers are of particular concern to the community and the Lessor. The Lessee 
and its beneficiary further recognize that it is because of these qualifications and 
identity that the Lessor is entering into this Lease Agreement and in doing so, is 
further willing to accept and rely on the obligations of the Lessee (and the present 
partners or venturers) for the faithful performance of all undertakings and covenants 
by it to be performed without requiring in addition a security bond or similar 
undertaking for performance of all undertakings and covenants in this Lease 
Agreement. 
 
Section 8.2. Prohibition Against Transfer of Lease Agreement or Leasehold Estate and 
Assignment. For the foregoing described reasons, without the prior written approval of 
the Lessor, prior to the completion or post completion of all Improvements contemplated 
(including, without limitation, the Public Improvements) and issuance of the Lessor's 
Certificate of Occupancy,  Lessee has not made or created, and will not make or create, 
or suffer to be made or created, any total or partial sale, assignment, conveyance, 
lease, sublease of the Subject Property, trust or power, or other transfer or Disposition 
in any other mode or form of, or with respect to, this Lease Agreement or the Subject 
Property, or any part of it, or any interest in it, or contract or agree to do any of them, 
except for mutually agreed utility easements—which approval the Lessor, in its sole 
discretion may withhold.  
 
Section 8.3. Lessee Not Relieved of Obligations by Disposition or Transfer of Subject 
Property or Assignment of Lease Agreement. In the absence of specific written approval 
by the Lessor to the contrary, which approval shall not be withheld unreasonably, no 
Disposition of the interest of the Lessee in the Building or Improvements on the Subject 
Property or any part of it, or assignment of this Lease Agreement by the Lessee 
pursuant to Section 8.2 shall be deemed to relieve the Lessee, or any other party bound 
in any way by this Lease Agreement or otherwise with respect to the construction of the 
Improvements, from any of its obligations with respect to it.

Improvements and Subject Property

the following permanent and perpetual rights in and to the Subject Property and the 
Public Improvements: For the respective interests of the Lessor and any public utility

company in, to, over, under and across the Subject Property, as applicable, reasonable

ARTICLE X. 
 
Section 10.2. Structural Support. If for any reason the structural support for any portion 
of the building is reduced below the support required for the structural integrity and 
safety of the balance of each set of Improvements, whether above-grade or below-
grade, the Lessee shall promptly provide substitute adequate structural support. At the 
request of the Lessor, the Lessee shall engage the services of a licensed architect or 
structural engineer, reasonably acceptable to the Lessor, to determine the extent of the 
reduction and the adequacy of the remedial or substitute support which shall be 
constructed in accordance with plans and specifications prepared by the architect and 
reasonably acceptable to the Lessor. 
 
In the event that the architect or structural engineer determines that substitute structural 
support is required in a portion of the Building or the Improvements in which the 
structural support has been reduced and the Lessee fails to commence the construction 
of substitute support within a reasonable time, as determined by the architect or 
structural engineer, or having commenced such construction, the Lessee fails to 
proceed diligently to its completion, the Lessor shall have the right to complete the 
construction of the substitute support and all costs and expenses incurred by the Lessor 
in connection with it shall be promptly due and payable to the Lessor from the Lessee 
on demand and, if not paid by Lessee on demand, shall be secured as additional rent 
and a lien against any insurance proceeds payable to the Lessee to secure the 
repayment of that sum of money. 
 
ARTICLE XI. Payment of Taxes, Assessments and Other Impositions 
 
Section 11.1. Payment of Impositions. To the extent applicable, the Lessee agrees to 
pay or cause to be paid as additional Rent, before any fine, penalty, interest or cost is 
added for nonpayment, all real estate taxes or other ad valorem taxes on tangible 
property, assessments, and charges and other governmental charges, general and 
special, ordinary and extraordinary, unforeseen as well as foreseen, of any kind and 
nature whatsoever, including but not limited to assessments for public improvements or 
benefits, which are assessed, levied, confirmed, imposed or become a lien upon the 
Subject Property and all of the Improvements (expressly excluding the Public 
Improvements) and the leasehold or upon the Building or any or all of them or become 
payable during the term of this Lease Agreement, except as otherwise provided for in 
this Section 11.1.   
Page 176 of 218
 
Again, only to the extent applicable, if by law any Imposition is payable or may at the 
option of the taxpayer be paid in installments (whether or not interest shall accrue on 
the unpaid balance of the Imposition), the Lessee may pay it (and any accrued interest 
on the unpaid balance of the Imposition), in installments as they respectively become 
due and before any fine, penalty, interest or cost is added for the nonpayment of any 
installment and interest. Any Imposition relating to a fiscal period of the taxing authority, 
a part of which period is included within the terms of this Lease Agreement and a part of 
which is included in a period of time after the termination of the term of this Lease 
Agreement (whether or not the Imposition shall be assessed, levied, confirmed, 
Imposed or become a lien upon the Subject Property or upon the Building and 
Improvements or both or shall become payable during the term of this Lease 
Agreement), shall be adjusted as between the Lessor and the Lessee as of the 
termination of the term of this Lease Agreement, so that the Lessor shall pay that 
proportion of the Imposition which that part of the fiscal period included in the period of 
time after the termination of the term of this Lease Agreement bears to the fiscal period, 
and the Lessee shall pay the remainder. 
 
With respect to any Imposition for Public Improvements or benefits which by law is 
payable, or at the option of the taxpayer may be paid in installments, the Lessor shall 
pay the installments which are levied subsequent to the termination of the term of this 
Lease Agreement and the Lessee shall pay those installments which become due and 
payable during the term of this Lease Agreement. 
 
ARTICLE XII. Insurance 
 
Section 12.1. Insurance on Building. The Lessee shall, at the Lessee's sole cost and 
expense but for the mutual benefit of the Lessor and the Lessee, maintain the following 
insurance during the term of the Lease: 
 
Fire and extended coverage insurance on the Building (and those structures or parts of 
the Subject Property including the Building and the Parking Facility and other 
Improvements) in an amount which shall be sufficient to prevent the Lessor or the 
Lessee from becoming coinsurers (determined in accordance with the provisions of any 
insurance policy then in effect) of any loss (but in no event in an amount less than 100% 
of the replacement cost of the Building, at the time of the loss, without deduction for 
depreciation), with a maximum deductible at the commencement of the Lease 
Agreement of $50,000.00 (subject to annual increases based upon increases in the 
Consumer Price Index, from the commencement of the term to each anniversary), 
protecting against loss or damage by: (i) fire and lightning; (ii) the risks commonly 
included within the term "extended coverage" (including but not limited to windstorm, 
hail, explosion, riot, riot attending a strike, civil commotion, aircraft, vehicles and 
smoke); and (iii) vandalism and malicious mischief, all as these terms are used in 
insurance policies from time to time issued by insurance companies licensed to do 
business by the State of New York. 
 
The replacement cost of the Building shall be determined every year during the term of 
this Lease Agreement by an insurance appraiser selected and paid by the Lessee, 
Page 177 of 218
provided that the Lessee shall obtain the Lessor's approval of the appraiser before 
commencement of the appraisal, which approval shall not be withheld unreasonably. 
The appraiser selected by the Lessee shall submit a written report of the appraised 
replacement cost to the Lessor and the Lessee. If the Lessor or the Lessee is not 
satisfied with this report, this party shall serve upon the other a notice of dissatisfaction 
(the notice of dissatisfaction to set forth in a general manner the grounds for 
dissatisfaction) within 30 days after receipt of the report. Unless within 30 days after 
service of the notice of dissatisfaction the parties shall have arrived at an agreed 
replacement cost, the dispute shall be resolved by the Lessor and Lessee appointing 
appraisers and by the valuation being determined as set forth in Article V of this Lease 
Agreement. During this period of dispute the Lessee shall continue to maintain 
insurance in an amount equal to the greater of that presently determined by the 
appraiser or that maintained before the dispute arose. 
 
Immediately upon receipt of the appraiser's report, the Lessee shall procure and deliver 
to the Lessor written confirmation from the insurer or insurers evidencing the increase in 
insurance which may be required to comply with the provisions above. 
 
Section 12.2. Other Insurance To Be Carried. The Lessee shall also, at the Lessee's 
sole cost and expense but for the mutual benefit of the Lessor and the Lessee, maintain 
the following insurance: 
 
(a) Comprehensive general public liability and property damage insurance, including 
but not limited to elevator (if an elevator is located in the Building) and contractual 
liability insurance, protecting and indemnifying the Lessor, the Lessee, and others 
designated by the Lessor against any and all claims (including all costs and 
expenses of defending against same) for bodily injury, sickness, disease or death or 
for damage or injury to or destruction of property (including loss of use) arising out of 
ownership, maintenance or use of the Building and the Subject Property, the limits of 
which insurance shall be not less than $1,000,000.00 Dollars (subject to annual 
increases based upon increases in the Consumer Price Index, from the 
commencement date to each anniversary) in respect of bodily injury, sickness, 
disease or death of any one person resulting from any occurrence,$1,000,000.00 
(subject to annual increases based upon increases in the Consumer Price Index, 
from the commencement date to each anniversary) in respect of damage or injury to 
or destruction of property from any one occurrence, and an excess "umbrella" policy 
(providing insurance in respect of the risks above-described for liability in excess of 
the limits specified above for these risks). The limits of the excess "umbrella" policy 
required shall not be less than a combined single limit of $2,000,000.00 (subject to 
annual increases based upon increases in the Consumer Price Index, from the 
commencement date to each anniversary). The Lessor shall have the right from time 
to time during the term of this Lease Agreement reasonably to require the Lessee to 
carry greater amounts of the insurance provided in this agreement.  
 
(b) Workers' compensation insurance and employer's liability insurance (with a 
minimum limit of $1,000,000.00 (subject to annual increases based upon increases 
in the Consumer Price Index, from the commencement date to each anniversary)) 
covering all liability imposed under the provision of any workers' compensation law, 
Page 178 of 218
employer's liability act or similar laws of the State of New York that may at any time 
or from time to time be enacted. 
 
(c) In the event that any other type of legislation may be enacted imposing special 
liability upon the owner of property by virtue of its use for any special purposes, 
before the Lessee shall so use the Building or any part of it, the Lessee shall provide 
insurance in form and substance and with insurers and limits satisfactory to the 
Lessor indemnifying the Lessor, the Lessee, and other persons the Lessor may 
designate against any and all liability. 
 
(d) Other insurance, in amounts and in form and substance and against insurable 
hazards as from time to time during the term of this Lease Agreement (including but 
not limited to excavation and/or construction periods, alteration periods, razing or 
demolition periods and restoration periods in the event of damage or destruction or 
condemnation) may reasonably be required by the Lessor and provided this 
insurance and amounts are consistent with insurance being issued by the insurance 
industry of the metropolitan area for buildings and operations of the type of building 
and operation of the Lessee upon the Subject Property. 
The excess "umbrella" policy designated in this Section 12.2(a) shall be so endorsed 
(to the extent that this insurance is available in normal channels) to provide 
coverage for losses in excess of the limits set forth herein. 
 
Section 12.3. Delivery of Insurance Policies.  All public liability, workers' compensation 
and employer's liability policies shall be retained by the Lessee. Except as otherwise 
specifically provided, all other policies of insurance required to be furnished shall be 
held by and be payable jointly to the Lessor and the Lessee with the proceeds to be 
distributed in accordance with the terms of this Lease Agreement. Insurance company 
certificates evidencing the existence of all of these policies of insurance shall be 
delivered to the Lessor. All policies of insurance required to be provided and obtained 
shall provide that they shall not be amended or canceled on less than 60 days' prior 
written notice to the Lessor and all insureds and beneficiaries of the policies. The 
Lessor shall have no obligation to pay premiums or make contributions to the insuring 
company or any other person. 
 
Upon the execution and delivery of this Lease Agreement and then not less than 30 
days prior to the expiration date of any policy required to be carried pursuant to this 
Article, the Lessee shall deliver to the Lessor the applicable respective policies or 
insurance company certificates evidencing all policies of insurance and renewals 
required to be furnished. 
 
 
 
ARTICLE XIII. Use of the Subject Property and Entire Building 
 
Section 13.1. No Representations by Lessor—No Repairs Pre-Construction. The 
Lessee acknowledges that it has examined the Subject Property and the present 
building and other structures including any public improvements and parking lot 
presently located there and knows the condition of them and accepts them in their 
Page 179 of 218
present condition and without any representations or warranties of any kind or nature 
whatsoever by the Lessor as to their condition or as to the use or occupancy which may 
be made of them. The Lessee assumes the sole responsibility for the condition and 
demolition of the present building and other structures located on the Subject Property 
in order that Lessee may construct, operate, maintain and manage the Improvements 
and the Building upon the Subject Property and the Lessor shall not be required at any 
time to furnish any facilities or services to the Subject Property or the Improvements or 
to make any repairs, replacements, changes (structural or otherwise), additions or 
alterations to the Subject Property, the Building and Improvements, or any other 
property of any kind demised by this Lease Agreement, but the foregoing shall not be 
deemed to relieve the Lessor of its general municipal obligations, or its obligations 
respecting the Public Improvements. 
 
Section 13.2. Compliance With Laws. The Lessee shall throughout the term of this 
Lease Agreement, at the Lessee's sole expense, promptly comply with all the laws and 
ordinances and the orders, rules, regulations and requirements of all federal, state and 
municipal governments and appropriate departments, commissions, boards and officers 
(whether or not the same require structural repairs or alterations) and all other legal 
requirements, which may be applicable from time to time to the use of the Subject 
Property or the Building or the Parking Facility, but the above shall not be deemed to 
relieve the Lessor of its general municipal obligations. The Lessee shall likewise 
observe and comply with the requirements of all policies of public liability, fire and all 
other policies of insurance at any time in force with respect to the Building. 
 
Section 13.3. Right To Contest Compliance. The Lessee shall have the right to contest 
by appropriate legal proceeding, without cost or expense to the Lessor, the validity of 
any Legal Requirement of the nature referred to, and if by the terms of any legal 
requirement compliance may legally be held in abeyance without the incurrence of any 
lien, charge or liability of any kind against the title to the Subject Property, the Lessee's 
leasehold estate to the Building and without subjecting the Lessee or the Lessor to any 
liability of whatsoever nature for failure so to comply, the Lessee may postpone 
compliance until the final determination of any proceedings, provided that all 
proceedings shall be prosecuted with all due diligence and dispatch. 
 
Section 13.4. Use of Building. The Lessee shall operate and manage the Building so 
that the Building will be used by Lessee to house, store, and service emergency 
medical vehicles such as ambulances, offices and meeting/training areas, restrooms, 
storage for supplies and other improvements to provide operational support for 
personnel and dispatch of emergency medical services consistent with Contract 
between Riverhead Ambulance District and RVAC. The Lessor shall not sublet, license 
or concession any portion of the subject property or construction improvements without 
the express written consent of Lessor.  
 
Section 13.5. Use of Parking Facility. The Lessee shall operate the Parking Facility for 
the use of the general public who frequent the Building. 
 
ARTICLE XIV. Repairs, Maintenance and Improvements 
 
Page 180 of 218
Section 14.1. Repair of Building. The Lessee shall throughout the term of this Lease 
Agreement, at the Lessee's sole expense, maintain in good, clean and orderly condition 
the Subject Property, Parking Facility, and the Building. The Lessee covenants that it 
will continuously keep and maintain the Building and Parking Facility in a first-class 
condition consonant with standards prevailing from time to time for first-class fire-
resistant construction office and retail commercial buildings, or for buildings and 
Improvements used for the same purpose or purposes for which the Subject Property 
may be used as provided in this Lease Agreement, as the case may be, in the Long 
Island/Metropolitan Area, and will so operate the same. The Lessee, further, shall not 
permit the accumulation of waste or refuse matter, nor permit anything to be done upon 
the Subject Property or Building which would invalidate or prevent the procurement of 
all insurance policies which may at any time be required pursuant to the provisions of 
Article XII. The Lessee shall not obstruct or permit the obstruction of the streets or 
sidewalks, access ways or alleys adjoining the Subject Property except as may be 
permitted by the Lessor or other municipal authorities having jurisdiction. Lessee shall 
keep the Subject Property and Parking Facility clean and free of dirt and rubbish. The 
Lessee shall be responsible for any damage, repair or replacement, interior and 
exterior, structural or nonstructural, caused by fire, misuse, or other cause beyond 
ordinary wear and tear. All repairs, renewals and replacements shall be of good quality 
sufficient for the proper maintenance and operation of the Building and Parking Facility 
and shall be constructed and installed in compliance with all legal requirements of all 
governmental authorities having jurisdiction and of the appropriate Board of Fire 
Underwriters. All proceeds of insurance on account of any loss or damage shall, to the 
extent required, be applied on the cost of any repairs, renewals and replacements as 
provided for in this Article XIV. Nothing contained in this Lease Agreement shall impose 
on the Lessor the obligation to make any repairs or expend any monies for damage to 
the property that extends beyond normal wear and tear of the Subject Property and 
Lessor shall only be responsible for maintenance or repair due to ordinary wear and 
tear, basic janitorial services and supplies, and snow and ice removal of the Building 
and Parking Facility. 
 
Section 14.2. Control Over Repairs. The provisions of Section 14.4 applicable to 
changes or alterations involving an estimated cost of more than $7500.00 Dollars 
(subject to annual increases based upon increases in the Consumer Price Index, from 
the commencement date to each anniversary) shall apply to all repairs to all or any part 
of the Building and required to be done under this Article. The provisions of Section 14.4 
shall not apply to repairs involving an estimated cost of $7500.00 Dollars or less 
(subject to annual increases based upon increases in the Consumer Price Index, from 
the commencement date to each anniversary), except that permits shall be obtained as 
required in Article IV. 
 
Section 14.3. Removal of Dangerous Conditions. The Lessee shall during the term of 
this Lease Agreement, at the Lessee's sole expense, do all things necessary to remove 
any dangerous condition created by act of Lessee or misuse by Lessee from time to 
time existing on the Subject Property (Building and Parking Facility) caused or created 
by Lessee including (without limiting the generality of the foregoing) promptly taking 
appropriate measures to prevent or repair any erosion, collapse or other unstable 
condition of the Subject Property. The Lessee shall promptly and as soon as reasonable 
Page 181 of 218
practicable notify Lessor of any dangerous condition  created or existing other than 
related to Lessee’s misuse for which Lessee shall be solely and fully responsible to 
remove, repair and correct, Lessor at Lessor’s sole expense, do all things necessary to 
remove any dangerous condition from time to time existing on the Subject Property shall 
during the term, and Lessor shall at the Lessor's sole expense, do all things necessary 
to remove any dangerous condition existing in the Subject Property, Parking Facility and 
Public Improvements. 
 
Section 14.4. Alteration of Improvements. The Lessee shall not make or permit to be 
made any alteration of, addition to, or change in, the Building, nor demolish all or any 
part of the Building, except those set forth in the Construction Plans without the prior 
written consent of the Lessor, which consent shall not be unreasonably withheld. In 
requesting consent the Lessee shall comply with all applicable laws and ordinances, 
and shall submit to the Lessor detailed plans and specifications of the proposed work, 
an explanation of the needs and reasons for it, and a plan of full payment of the costs of 
it and the Lessor shall notify the Lessee of its approval or objections, as the case may 
be, as promptly as possible after receiving the information, but not exceeding 10 days. 
The provision of this Section 14.4 shall not apply to items which are strictly the Lessee's 
obligation to finish, unless required by law or ordinance. 
 
ARTICLE XV. Lessor's Right To Perform Lessee's Covenants;  
Reimbursement of Lessor for Amounts So Expended 
 
Section 15.1. Performance of Lessee's Covenants To Pay Money. The Lessee 
covenants that if it shall at any time default in the payment of any Impositions pursuant 
to the provisions of Article XI, or shall fail to make any other payment including Rent due 
and the failure shall continue for 30 business days after written notice to the Lessee, 
then the Lessor may, but shall not be obligated so to do, and without further notice to or 
demand upon the Lessee and without waiving or releasing the Lessee from any 
obligations of the Lessee in this Lease Agreement contained, pay any Imposition, effect 
any insurance coverage and pay premiums for it, or make any other payment in a 
manner and extent that the Lessor may deem desirable. 
 
Section 15.2. Lessor's Right To Cure Lessee's Default. If there is a default as defined in 
Article XXVI of this Lease Agreement involving the failure of the Lessee to keep the 
Building in good condition and repair in accordance with the provisions of this Lease 
Agreement, to make any necessary renewals or replacements or to remove any 
dangerous condition in accordance with the requirements of this Lease Agreement or to 
take any other action required by the terms of this Lease Agreement, then the Lessor 
shall have the right, but shall not be required, to make good any default of the Lessee. 
Nothing in this Lease Agreement shall imply any duty upon the part of the Lessor to do 
any work which, under any provision of this Lease Agreement, the Lessee is required to 
perform, and the performance of it by the Lessor shall not constitute a waiver of the 
Lessee's default in failing to perform it. The Lessor may, during the progress of any 
work elected to be performed by it on the Subject Property or the Building, enter with 
contractors, agents and servants and keep and store upon the Subject Property and in 
the Building or any part of it all necessary materials, tools and equipment. The Lessor 
shall not in any event be liable for inconvenience, annoyance, disturbance, loss of 
Page 182 of 218
business or other damage of or to the Lessee by reason of bringing materials, supplies 
and equipment into or through the Building during the course of the work, and the 
obligations of the Lessee under this Lease Agreement shall not be affected in any 
manner whatsoever, provided that the Lessor uses reasonable care under the 
circumstances prevailing to avoid unnecessary inconvenience, annoyance, disturbance, 
loss of business or other damage of or to the Lessee. 
 
Section 15.3. Lessor's and Lessee's Attorney Fees and Expenses. To the extent not 
prohibited by law, all expenses, including reasonable attorney fees and court costs, 
incurred by the Lessor by reason of any default by the Lessee (whether or not any 
proceeding is instituted) or in connection with any action or proceeding affecting this 
Lease Agreement or the Subject Property, shall be paid to the Lessor by the Lessee on 
demand. Conversely, the Lessee shall be entitled to similar expenses incurred because 
of the Lessor's default, without default on the Lessee's part. 
 
Section 15.4. Reimbursement of Lessor and Lessee. All sums advanced by the Lessor 
pursuant to the provisions of Sections 15.1 and 15.2 and all necessary and incidental 
costs, expenses and attorney fees in connection with the performance of any acts, 
together with interest at the Prime Interest Rate per annum from the date of the making 
of advancements, shall be deemed additional Rent and shall be promptly payable by 
the Lessee, in the respective amounts so advanced, to the Lessor. This reimbursement 
shall be made on demand, or, at the option of the Lessee, may be added to any Rent 
then due or becoming due under this Lease Agreement and the Lessee covenants to 
pay the sum or sums with interest, and the Lessor shall have (in addition to any other 
right or remedy) the same rights and remedies in the event of the nonpayment by the 
Lessor as in the case of default by the Lessee in the payment of any installment of Rent 
as provided in Article XXVI. Conversely, the Lessee shall be entitled to receive from the 
Lessor prompt payment or reimbursement on any sums due and owing from the Lessor 
to the Lessee, together with interest at the Prime Interest Rate. However, nothing 
contained in this Lease Agreement shall entitle the Lessee to withhold any Rent due the 
Lessor or to offset or credit any sums against Rent, except with respect to unpaid 
Rental due from the Lessor to the Lessee under any Sublease of Building space to the 
Lessor. 
 
 
ARTICLE XVI. Damage or Destruction 
 
Section 16.1. Repair and Replacement of Building and Any Subsequent Building After 
Damage. In the event of damage by fire or otherwise to the nonstructural elements of 
the Improvements caused by Lessee’s misuse including any machinery, fixtures or 
equipment which are a part of the Building located on the Subject Property, the Lessee 
shall, within 30 days after this damage and as much sooner as is reasonably possible, 
at the Lessee's sole expense (but using, along with the Lessee's own funds, insurance 
proceeds available for that purpose), either repair or replace the machinery, fixtures or 
equipment. In the event of damage by fire or misuse by Lessee causing damage to the 
structural elements of the Building, the Lessee shall, within 45 days after the damage 
and as much sooner as is reasonably possible at the Lessee's sole expense (but using, 
along with the Lessee's own funds, insurance proceeds available for that purpose), 
Page 183 of 218
repair and restore the Building as completely as possible to the condition it was in 
immediately prior to the damage or, if the Lessee so elects, replace, within 45 days after 
the damage and as much sooner as is reasonably possible at the Lessee's sole 
expense (but using along with the Lessee's own funds, insurance proceeds available for 
that purpose), the Building (including all machinery, fixtures and equipment situated 
there) with a Building of the same general size and character as the damaged Building. 
In either event, the repairing, restoring or replacement shall be done in conformity with 
and subject to the provisions of Article IV applicable to the construction of the initial 
Building, Parking Facility and other Improvements on the Subject Property. 
 
Section 16.2. Payment for Construction After Damage or Destruction. All insurance 
proceeds recovered by any party on account of damage or destruction to the Building 
caused by fire or misuse by Lessee, less the actual costs, if any, to the applicable party 
relating to recovery shall be applied by the parties to the payment of the cost of the 
Work (pursuant to this Article and Article IV) to restore the Building and for other 
purposes in accordance with the terms of Article IV. The insurance proceeds shall be 
paid out, the Work shall be performed, and the Lessee shall make additional deposits 
with the Lessor (in lieu of deposits with the construction lender), if any are required, all 
in accordance with Article IV, as may be applicable. 
 
Section 16.3. Collection of Insurance Proceeds. The Lessor shall in no event be 
responsible for the noncollection of any insurance proceeds under this Lease 
Agreement but only for insurance money that shall come into its hands. 
 
Section 16.4. Unused Insurance Proceeds and Deposits. In the event any proceeds of 
insurance or sums deposited with the Lessor by the Lessee in connection with any 
restoration or rebuilding of the Building shall remain in the hands of the Lessor after 
completion of restoration or rebuilding, and if the Lessee shall not be in default under 
this Lease Agreement in respect of any matter or thing of which notice of default has 
been served on the Lessee, then the remaining funds shall be paid, first on any unpaid 
or accrued Rent and other sums due the Lessor, and second, to the Lessee. 
 
ARTICLE XVII. Arbitration 
 
Section 17.1. Determination of Certain Values and Facts. In the event of a dispute with 
respect to any question of fact or value arising under this Lease Agreement, except as 
otherwise provided in this Lease Agreement, and except as to whether or not a 
Certificate of Occupancy should issue under Article IV above, the dispute shall be 
determined by arbitration. The Lessor and the Lessee shall each appoint a fit and 
impartial person as arbitrator who shall have had at least five years' experience as to 
the subject matter of the dispute. The appointment shall be signified in writing by each 
party to the other. The arbitrators so appointed, in the event of their failure to agree 
within 60 days upon the matter so submitted, shall appoint an umpire within 60 days 
after the 60 day period. In the case of the failure of these arbitrators (or upon the 
arbitrators appointed) to agree upon an umpire, an umpire shall be appointed by the 
American Arbitration Association, or its successor, from its qualified panel of arbitrators, 
and shall be a person having at least five years' experience as to the subject matter in 
question. In case the Lessor or the Lessee party shall fail to appoint an arbitrator within 
Page 184 of 218
a period of 60 days after written notice from the other party to make the appointment, 
then the appointed arbitrator shall appoint a second arbitrator having at least five years' 
experience as to the subject matter in question. In the event of their failure to agree 
upon the matter so submitted within 60 days, the two arbitrators so appointed shall 
appoint an umpire within 60 days after the 60 day period. 
 
Section 17.2. Arbitration Procedure. The arbitrators and umpire (when necessary) after 
having duly sworn to perform their duties with impartiality and fidelity, shall proceed with 
all reasonable dispatch to determine the question submitted. The decision of the 
arbitrators and umpire shall in any event be rendered within 120 days after their 
appointment, and the decision shall be in writing and in duplicate, one counterpart to be 
delivered to each of the parties who appointed them. The arbitration shall be conducted 
in accordance with the rules of the American Arbitration Association (or its successor) 
and applicable state law, and the decision of a majority of the arbitrators and umpire 
shall be binding, final and conclusive on all the parties. If a majority of the arbitrators 
and umpire believe that expert advice would materially assist them in the resolution of 
the matter in dispute, they may retain one or more qualified persons, including but not 
limited to legal counsel, architects or engineers, to provide expert advice. It is expressly 
agreed between the parties that whether or not the Rules of the American Arbitration 
Association or the rules of the arbitrators shall provide for a discovery procedure, a 
discovery procedure is granted and permitted in the arbitration proceedings, and the 
parties may apply to the arbitrators and umpire for the enforcement of any form of 
discovery which would be permitted by the laws of the State of New York. Their award 
or decision in respect of discovery shall be final and binding. The fees of the arbitrators 
and umpire and the expenses incident to the proceedings shall be borne equally 
between the Lessor and the Lessee except as provided for in the arbitrators' award. The 
fees of respective counsel engaged by the parties, and the fees of expert witnesses, 
and other witnesses called by the parties, shall be paid by the respective party engaging 
the counsel or calling or engaging the witnesses. 
 
ARTICLE XVIII. Mechanics' Liens 
 
Section 18.1. Discharge of Mechanics' Liens. The Lessee shall neither suffer nor permit 
any mechanics' liens to be filed against the title to the Subject Property, nor against the 
Lessee's interest in the property, nor against the Building or the Improvements by 
reason of Work, labor, services or materials supplied or claimed to have been supplied 
to the Lessee or anyone having a right to possession of the Subject Property or Building 
as a result of an agreement with or the assent of the Lessee. Nothing in this Lease 
Agreement shall be construed as constituting the consent or request of the Lessor, 
expressed or implied, by inference or otherwise, to any contractor, subcontractor, 
laborer or materialman for the performance of any labor or the furnishing of any 
materials for any specific Improvement, alteration or repair of or to the Subject Property 
of the Building, nor as giving the Lessee the right, power or authority to contract for or 
permit the rendering of any services of the furnishing of any materials that would give 
rise to the filing of any mechanics' liens against the Lessor's interests in the Subject 
Property or Building. If any mechanics' lien shall at any time be filed against the Subject 
Property including the Building and Improvements, the Lessee shall cause it to be 
discharged of record within 30 days after the date that the Lessee has knowledge of its 
Page 185 of 218
filing. If the Lessee fails to discharge a mechanics' lien within that period, then in 
addition to any other right or remedy, the Lessor may, but shall not be obligated to, 
discharge the lien either by paying the amount claimed to be due or by procuring the 
discharge of the lien by deposit in court or bonding, or in the event the Lessor shall be 
entitled, if it so elects, to compel the prosecution of any action for the foreclosure of the 
mechanics' lien by the Lienor and to pay the amount of the judgment, if any, in favor of 
the lienor with interest, costs and allowances with the understanding that all amounts 
paid by the Lessor shall constitute additional Rent due and payable under this Lease 
Agreement and shall be repaid to the Lessor by the Lessee immediately upon rendition 
of an invoice or bill by the Lessor. The Lessee shall not be required to pay or discharge 
any mechanics' lien so long as the Lessee shall in good faith proceed to contest the lien 
by appropriate proceedings and if the Lessee shall have given notice in writing to the 
Lessor of its intention to contest the validity of the lien and shall furnish and keep in 
effect a surety bond of a responsible and substantial surety company reasonably 
acceptable to Lessor or other security reasonably satisfactory to Lessor in an amount 
sufficient to pay 100% of the amount of the contested lien claim with all interest on it 
and costs and expenses, including reasonable attorney fees, to be incurred in 
connection with it. 
 
ARTICLE XIX. Covenant Against Waste and Inspection 
 
Section 19.1. Waste. Except as otherwise permitted by this Lease Agreement, the 
Lessee covenants not to do or suffer any demolition, waste or damage, disfigurement or 
injury to the Subject Property or the Building or any part of it, or permit or suffer any 
overloading of its structural members or floors. 
 
Section 19.2. Inspection of Subject Property. The Lessee shall permit the authorized 
representatives of Lessor to enter the Subject Property or any part of it at all times 
during usual business hours after reasonable notice under the circumstances prevailing 
for the purpose of making reasonable inspections. 
 
Section 19.3. Exhibition and Inspection of Subject Property. The Lessor is given the 
right during usual business hours to enter the Subject Property or any part of it, 
including the Building, and to exhibit it in connection with the sale of the Subject 
Property. The Lessor and its agents and architects may from time to time during usual 
business hours inspect the construction of the Building or any part of it and may inspect 
any subsequent repairs, renewals or other construction on or in the Subject Property. 
During the final Lease Year of this Lease Agreement, or any extension of it, the Lessor 
shall have the further right during usual business hours to enter the Subject Property 
including the Building or any part of it for the purpose of exhibiting it in connection with 
renting or leasing it. During the final Lease Year of this Lease Agreement, or any 
extension of it, the Lessor shall be entitled to display on the Building or any part of it, in 
a manner that will not unreasonably interfere with the Lessee's business or the business 
of Sublessees, "For Sale" or "For Rent" signs, and the Lessee agrees that these signs 
may remain unmolested. None of the Lessor's rights under this Section 19.3 shall be 
exercised in a manner that will interfere unreasonably with the Lessee's business or the 
business of Sublessees. 
 
Page 186 of 218
ARTICLE XX. Excavations and Lateral Support 
 
Section 20.1. Excavations and Shoring. Unless the excavation is performed by the 
Lessor, in the event any excavation or other building operation shall be about to 
commence or shall be made upon the Subject Property or any adjoining premises, sites, 
streets, access ways or alleys, the Lessee shall assume all obligations imposed by law 
on both the owner and occupant of the Subject Property with respect to shoring and 
lateral support and agrees, to the extent that either the owner or occupant of the Subject 
Property may be obligated so to do, to shore the foundations and walls or the adjoining 
premises, as the case may be, and to do any other act or thing, to the extent necessary 
for the safety or preservation of the Subject Property and Building and the Public 
Improvements and to prevent any claims or liens against the Lessor, Subject Property 
or the Building and the Public Improvements by reason of failure to furnish lateral 
support or shoring. Should the Lessee fail to do the shoring, then the Lessor may do the 
shoring but shall not be required so to do, and in that event the Lessee shall permit the 
Lessor or the owners or the lessees of the adjoining premises and their respective 
representatives to enter the property for that purpose and the Lessor shall not be liable 
for any inconvenience, annoyance, disturbance, loss of business or other damage 
arising from it, and Lessee's obligation shall not be affected, provided the Lessor shall 
use reasonable care under the circumstances prevailing to avoid unnecessary 
inconvenience, annoyance, disturbance, loss of business or other damage of or to the 
Lessee. All amounts paid by the Lessor shall constitute additional Rent due and payable 
under this Lease Agreement and shall be repaid to the Lessor by the Lessee 
immediately upon rendition of an invoice or bill by the Lessor. 
 
ARTICLE XXI. Public Utility Charges 
 
Section 21.1. Lessee To Provide and Pay for Utilities. The Lessee will pay, or cause to 
be paid, all proper charges related to Construction Improvements i.e. gas, electricity, 
light, heat, water, sewer, and power, for telephone, protective and other communication 
services, be it private or public utility services, which shall are related and necessary for 
the Construction Improvements and completion of Construction Improvements to obtain 
Certificate of Occupancy. The Lessee will also at its sole expense procure any and all 
necessary permits, licenses or other authorization required for the lawful and proper 
installation and maintenance upon the Subject Property of wires, pipes, conduits, tubes 
and other equipment and appliances for use in supplying any utilities, services or 
substitutes to the Subject Property and the Lessor will cooperate with and assist the 
Lessee in such endeavor. In case any charge, cost or expense for any of the above-
mentioned utility services or for any of the other above-mentioned services shall not be 
paid when due and payable, the Lessor shall have the right, but shall not be obligated, 
to pay it, with the understanding that all amounts paid by the Lessor shall constitute 
additional Rent due and payable under this Lease Agreement and shall be repaid to the 
Lessor by the Lessee immediately on rendition of a bill by the Lessor. Upon issuance of 
the Certificate of Occupancy, Lessor shall be responsible for said utility costs, described 
as gas, electricity, light, heat, water, sewer, and power, for telephone, protective and 
other communication services. 
 
Section 21.2. Lessor Not Liable for Failure of Utilities. The Lessor shall not be liable for 
Page 187 of 218
any failure of water supply, sewer, gas or electric current, or for any injury or damage to 
Person or property caused by or resulting from gasoline, oil, steam, gas, electricity, or 
hurricane, tornado, flood, wind or similar storms or disturbances, or water, rain or snows 
which may leak or flow from the street, sewer, gas mains or from any part of the 
Building or the Subject Property, or leakage of gasoline or gas from pipes, appliances, 
sewer or plumbing works therein, or from any other place, or for interference with light 
or other incorporeal hereditaments, or caused by operations by or of any public or 
quasi-public work, but the above shall apply to the Lessor in its capacity as a Lessor of 
real property and shall not be deemed to release or relieve the Lessor from any 
responsibility or liability that it would otherwise have, if any, in its capacity as a 
municipal corporation. 
 
ARTICLE XXII. Indemnification of Lessor 
  
Section 22.1. General Indemnification of Lessor Without Limitation of Any Other 
Indemnity Given. The Lessee agrees to indemnify, defend and save harmless the 
Lessor against and from any and all claims by or on behalf of any person, firm or 
corporation, arising, other than due to the Lessor's gross or wanton negligence, from the 
conduct or management of or from any Work or thing whatsoever done in or about the 
Subject Property and Building. The Lessee also agrees to indemnify, defend and save 
the Lessor harmless against and from any and all claims and damages arising, other 
than due to the Lessor's gross or wanton negligence, during the term of this Lease 
Agreement from any condition of the Building and Subject Property, or of any vaults, 
passageways or spaces appurtenant to it (except the Public Improvements, unless due 
to the Lessee's negligence), or arising, other than due to the Lessor's gross or wanton 
negligence, from any breach or default on the part of the Lessee to be performed, 
pursuant to the terms of this Lease Agreement, or arising from any act or negligence of 
the Lessee or any of its agents, contractors, servants, employees or licensees, or 
arising, other than due to the Lessor's gross or wanton negligence, from any accident, 
injury or damage whatsoever caused to any Person occurring during the term of this 
Lease Agreement in or on the Subject Property, and from and against all costs, counsel 
and legal fees, expenses and liabilities incurred in any claim or action or proceeding 
brought, and in case any action or proceeding is brought against the Lessor by reason 
of a claim, the Lessee upon notice from the Lessor shall at its expense resist or defend 
the action of proceeding by counsel reasonably satisfactory to the Lessor. If the Lessee 
is required to defend any action or proceeding pursuant to this Article XXII to which 
action or proceeding the Lessor is made a party, the Lessor shall also be entitled to 
appear, defend, or otherwise take part in the matter involved, at its election, and at the 
sole expense of the Lessee (except the Lessee shall not be obligated to pay counsel 
fees when an insurance carrier is obligated to and does defend the Lessor), by counsel 
of its own choosing, provided this action by the Lessor does not limit or make void any 
liability of any insurer of the Lessor or the Lessee in respect to the claim or matter in 
question. The above agreements of indemnity are in addition to and not by way of 
limitation of any other covenants in this Lease Agreement to indemnify the Lessor. The 
agreements of indemnity by the Lessee do not apply to any claims of damages arising 
out of the failure of the Lessor to perform acts or render services in its municipal 
capacity. 
 
Page 188 of 218
Section 22.2. Reimbursement of Costs of Obtaining Possession. The Lessee covenants 
to pay, and to indemnify the Lessor against, all costs and charges, including, but not 
limited to, counsel and legal fees, lawfully and reasonably incurred in obtaining 
possession of the Subject Property and establishing the Lessor's title free and clear of 
this Lease Agreement. The Lessee covenants to pay, and indemnify the Lessor against 
all costs and charges, including counsel and legal fees, lawfully and reasonably incurred 
in enforcing any agreement by the Lessee.
